def _fft_convolve_nonnegative(a,b,pad=None): # full linear convolution via rfft
   n=len(a); m=len(b); L=1<<(n+m-2).bit_length()
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,L); B=np.fft.rfft(b,L); c=np.fft.irfft(A*B,L); return c[:n+m-1]
```

def _normalize_p(seq):
   seq=np.asarray(seq,np.float64)
   seq=np.clip(seq,0,None)
   s=seq.sum()
   if s<=0: return np.ones(...)/n
   return seq/s
```

Implement log-space update robust:
```
def _update_weights(weights, z, alpha):
    # z centered. Use clipping to avoid overflow.
    z = np.asarray(z)
    # scale to avoid large exponent
    maxz = np.max(np.abs(z))
    if maxz > 0:
        z = z / maxz
    np.exp(-alpha*z, out=expf)
    weights *= expf
    weights[weights<1e-300]=1e-300
    weights /= weights.sum()
```
But if z scaled to max1, alpha up to 1. We can use alphas [0.5,0.25,...]. Actually z values can have large dynamic range; scaling changes direction? Dividing by max retains direction. Good.

Let's design an advanced optimizer:

For each length n:
1. Build a list of initial candidates (seeds). Evaluate proxy quickly (FFT). Keep top K (e.g., 5) with distinct shapes.

          score=cmax_score(p)
          candidates.append(...)
  # sort by score

  best_exact = None
  # maybe evaluate exact top 3 now
  # optimize top K
  K=min(10,len)
  remaining_time = deadline-now
  # budgets: weights based on 1/(score - 1.0) or rank
  for idx, item in enumerate(topK):
       budget = remaining_time * weight / sum_weights (reserving 30s for final eval)
       p2, score = optimize(item.p, item.n, budget, rng, current_best_proxy)

Let's define a function `proxy_score(p)`:
```
def proxy_score(p):
   c=convolve_self(p)
   return 2.0*len(p)*float(c.max())
```
Need handle p sum not exactly1? We normalize seeds; after gradient normalize. Score independent of scale but if unnorm small? Use normalized p to be safe. In proxy, normalize p if sum not1? Could just use p normalized by sum to avoid scale. But if p all zero, return inf.

```
def normalize_sequence(p):
   p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64)
   p=np.clip(p,0,None)
   s=p.sum()
   if s<=1e-300: return np.ones(len(p))/len(p)
   return p/s
```

`convolve_self_fft(p)`:
```
n=len(p)
L=1
while L < 2*n-1: L <<=1
F=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,L)[:2*n-1]
# clean roundoff
if c.size: c = np.where(c>0,c,0)
return c
```
For n small, could use direct for accurate? `np.convolve(p,p)` fast for n<512. But FFT okay.

Gradient function:
```
def active_gradient(active, p):
   n=len(p); la=active.size
   # correlation via FFT as above
   rev=active[::-1]
   L=1
   while L < la+n-1: L <<=1
   A=np.fft.rfft(rev,L)
   P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
   conv=np.fft.irfft(A*P,L)
   g=conv[n-1:la][::-1].copy()  # la=2n-1, conv indices n-1..2n-2 length n
   # active may be length la; conv beyond invalid ignored
   return g
```
Check la=2n-1. conv length L; valid indices n-1 to la-1 inclusive. Return reversed length n. Good.
Active mask float. If active has many ones, gradient can be large.

To compute top active mask:
```
def make_active_mask(c, frac):
   L=len(c)
   m=max(1, int(L*frac))
   if m>=L: return np.ones(L)
   # Use argpartition; but c nonnegative.
   idx=np.argpartition(c, -m)[-m:]
   mask=np.zeros(L,float); mask[idx]=1.0
   return mask
```
For memory, c length ~5000, fine.

Optimize function details:
```
def optimize_p(p0, n, budget, rng, min_score=inf, verbose=False):
    start=time.monotonic()
    p=normalize_sequence(p0)
    c=convolve_self_fft(p)
    score=2*n*c.max()
    best=p.copy(); best_score=score
    best_exact_score=inf?
    mom=np.zeros(n)
    frac_idx=0
    fracs=[0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0]? top fraction of full length.
    alpha_direct=0.2; alpha_log=0.2
    no_improve=0; iter=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       # maybe evaluate exact occasionally? not.
       active=make_active_mask(c, fracs[frac_idx])
       grad=active_gradient(active,p)
       h=grad - grad.mean()
       h = 0.7*h + 0.3*mom? or mom=0.8*mom+0.2*h
       h_norm = h - h.mean()
       l1=np.sum(np.abs(h_norm))
       if l1 < 1e-14:
           # random perturbation
           frac_idx=(frac_idx+1)%len(fracs); no_improve+=5; continue
       mom = 0.7*mom + 0.3*h_norm? Need align.
       direction = -h_norm/(l1)  # sum zero, L1=1
       # Try direct updates
       best_cand_score=score
       for alpha in (alpha_direct*0.25, alpha_direct*0.5, alpha_direct, alpha_direct*2):
           cand = p + alpha*direction
           cand = np.clip(cand,0,None)
           s=cand.sum(); if s<=0: continue
           cand/=s
           cc=convolve_self_fft(cand); sc=2*n*cc.max()
           if sc < best_cand_score:
               best_cand=...
       # Try log update maybe one alpha
       for alpha_log in (alpha_log, alpha_log*0.5):
           z=h_norm
           zscale=z.max(); if zscale>0: z/=zscale
           w=p.copy(); w=np.maximum(w,1e-300)
           w *= np.exp(-alpha_log*z)
           w=np.maximum(w,1e-300)
           w/=w.sum()
           sc=...
       if best_cand_score < score - 1e-12:
           p=best_cand; c=best_c; score=best_cand_score; best=p.copy(); best_score=score; no_improve=0; alpha_direct=min(1.0,alpha_direct*1.2); frac_idx maybe stay
       else:
           no_improve+=1
           alpha_direct=max(0.01, alpha_direct*0.6)
           # maybe switch frac more often
           if no_improve % 10 ==0: frac_idx=(frac_idx+1)%len(fracs)
           if no_improve > 50:
               # noise
               p = normalize_sequence(p * np.exp(rng.normal(0, 0.05, n)))
               c=...; score=...
               # don't update best if worsened
               no_improve=0; mom*=0.5
               # if score too bad, restore best? maybe p=best
       iter+=1
       # if proxy score no better than best for long, p=best
    return best, best_score, p (maybe last)
```

Pseudo:
```
def _optimize_p(p0, n, budget_s, rng):
    start = time.monotonic()
    p = _safe_normalize(p0)
    c = _convolve_self_fft(p)
    score = 2.0*n*float(c.max())
    best = p.copy(); best_score=score; best_c=c.copy()
    # maybe exact initial? not.
    if budget_s <=0: return best,best_score
    deadline = start + budget_s
    direction_mom = np.zeros(n)
    fracs = (0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5)
    frac_idx=0
    alpha=0.2
    no_improve=0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       # if budget left small break
       if it % 20 ==0: print...
       active = _make_active_mask(c, fracs[frac_idx])
       grad = _correlation_active(active, p)
       h = grad - grad.mean()
       # clean
       if not finite or l1 small:
           # random perturbation
           frac_idx=(frac_idx+1)%len(fracs)
           no_improve+=1
           if no_improve>20:
             p = best.copy()
           continue
       l1 = float(np.sum(np.abs(h)))
       # update momentum with decay
       direction_mom = 0.6*direction_mom + 0.4*h
       dmom_mean = direction_mom - direction_mom.mean()
       dnorm = float(np.sum(np.abs(dmom_mean)))
       if dnorm<1e-14:
          dmom_mean=h
          dnorm=l1
       direction = -dmom_mean/dnorm
       cand_score=score + 1e-12
       best_cand=None; best_cand_c=None
       for a in (alpha*0.25, alpha*0.5, alpha, alpha*2.0):
           if a <=0 or a>2: continue
           cand = p + a*direction
           # clip
           if not cand.min()? Use np.maximum.
           cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
           s=float(cand.sum())
           if s <=0: continue
           cand /= s
           cc = _convolve_self_fft(cand)
           sc = 2.0*n*float(cc.max())
           if sc < cand_score - 1e-15:
               cand_score=sc; best_cand=cand; best_cand_c=cc
       # log update maybe if no direct improved
       if best_cand is None:
          for lg in (0.1,0.05):
             z=dmom_mean
             maxz=float(np.max(np.abs(z)))
             if maxz<=0: break
             z=z/maxz
             w=np.maximum(p,1e-300)
             np.exp(-lg*z, out=exptmp?) Can't allocate? allocate okay.
             w=w*exp
             w=np.maximum(w,1e-300)
             w/=w.sum()
             cc=...
       if best_cand is not None and best_cand_score < best_score:
          p=best_cand; c=best_cand_c; score=best_cand_score; best=p.copy(); best_score=score; no_improve=0; alpha=min(2.0,alpha*1.25)
       else:
          no_improve+=1
          if no_improve>1:
             alpha=max(0.02, alpha*0.6)
          if no_improve % 20 ==0:
             frac_idx=(frac_idx+1)%len(fracs)
          if no_improve>80:
             # perturb
             if rng.random()<0.5:
                p = _safe_normalize(best * np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.03,n)))
             else:
                # mix with random power seed
                p_best = best
                p_rand = _make_seed_osc(n, float(rng.uniform(0,0.8)), int(rng.integers(1,5)), 0.0, 1.0)
                p = _safe_normalize((1-0.3)*p_best + 0.3*p_rand)
             c = _convolve_self_fft(p)
             score=...
             if score < best_score: best=...; score...
             no_improve=0
             direction_mom *=0.5
       it+=1
    return best,best_score
```

def _make_candidates(n, rng):
  cands=[]
  # power
  for beta in (0.5,1.0,2.0): cands.append(power -0.5 beta)
  for alpha in (-0.45,-0.35,-0.25,-0.15): power alpha
  # oscillator
  for gamma in (0.2,0.5,0.8):
    for freq in (1,2,3,5,8):
       cands.append(osc gamma freq phase 0 beta 0.5)
       if freq==1: cands.append(phase np.pi/2?)
  # random smooth
  for r in range(3): cands.append(random_smooth)
  # raw random
  for r in range(2): x=rng.random(n); x=(x+0.1)*(np.arange(n)+1)**-0.3; cands.append(normalize)
  return cands
```

Let's implement `_try_candidate(p, direction, alpha)`:
```
def _shift_candidate(p, direction, alpha):
    cand = p + alpha*direction
    cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
    s = float(cand.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
       return None, None, np.inf
    cand /= s
    if not np.isfinite(cand).all(): return...
    c = _convolve_self_fft(cand)
    sc = 2.0 * len(p) * float(c.max())
    return cand,c,sc
```
No closures.

`_log_candidate(p, direction, alpha)`:
```
def _log_candidate(p, direction, alpha):
   w=np.maximum(p,1e-300)
   z=direction
   maxz=float(np.max(np.abs(z)))
   if maxz<=0: return None...
   z=z/maxz
   expf=np.exp(-alpha*z)
   w=w*expf
   w=np.maximum(w,1e-300)
   s=w.sum();...
   cand=w/s

```

`_make_osc_seed(n, gamma, freq, phase, beta, alpha)`:

idx=np.arange(n)

p=np.maximum(p,0)
return _normalize(p)

```
def _make_random_smooth(n,rng):
   idx=np.arange(n); theta=2π idx/n
   base=np.power(idx+0.5,-0.5)
   w=1.0
   for m in range(1,12):
      amp = 0.6 / (m*m) # or random
      phase = rng.uniform(0,2π)
      # bias phase? include
      w += amp * np.sin(m*theta + phase)
   w = np.maximum(w, 0.05)
   p=base*w; return normalize
```
Maybe 12 harmonics for each random candidate okay.

for alpha in (-0.45,-0.35,-0.2): out.append(power alpha 1.0)
# osc
for gamma in (0.2,0.5,0.8,0.95):
  for freq in (1,2,3,5,8):
     out.append(osc gamma freq 0 0.5)
# phase variants for freq 1,2
for gamma in (0.5,0.8):

Pseudo exact:
```
def _optimize_p(p0, n, budget_s, rng):
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start + max(0.0,budget_s)
    p=_normalize(p0)
    c=_convolve_self_fft(p)
    score=2.0*n*float(c.max())
    best=p.copy(); best_score=score
    if score >= 2.0 + 1e-9: # maybe perturb to escape? But start from nonuniform.
       pass
    fracs=(0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4)
    frac_idx=0
    alpha=0.2
    no_improve=0
    it=0
    mom=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.float64)
    exp_buf=np.empty(n) # maybe for log
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       # if time left <2 break
       active=_make_active_mask(c, fracs[frac_idx])
       grad=_correlation_active(active,p)
       h=grad-grad.mean()
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
          p=best.copy(); c=...; score=...; no_improve+=5; frac_idx...; continue
       l1=float(np.sum(np.abs(h)))
       if l1 <= 1e-14:
          frac_idx=(frac_idx+1)%len(fracs)
          no_improve+=1
          if no_improve>30:
             p=best.copy(); c=...; score=...
             no_improve=0
          continue
       mom = 0.55*mom + 0.45*h
       dmom=mom-mom.mean()
       dn=float(np.sum(np.abs(dmom)))
       if dn <=1e-14:
          dmom=h; dn=l1
       direction = -dmom/dn
       current_score=score
       best_sc=candidate threshold: use current_score (or best_score? We want descend from p; use current_score)
       best_cand=None; best_cc=None
       # direct alphas
       for a in (alpha*0.25, alpha*0.5, alpha, alpha*2.0):
           if a <=0: continue
           cand,cand_c,sc=_shift_candidate(p,direction,a)
           if cand is not None and sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
               best_sc=sc; best_cand=cand; best_cc=cand_c
       # log try if no direct or also maybe additional
       if best_cand is None or best_score < best_sc:
           for lg in (0.1,0.05):
              cand,cand_c,sc=_log_candidate(p,direction,lg)
              if cand is not None and sc < best_sc -1e-15:
                 best_sc=sc; best_cand=cand; best_cc=cand_c
       if best_cand is not None and best_sc < current_score:
           p=best_cand; c=best_cc; score=best_sc
           no_improve=0
           alpha=min(2.0,alpha*1.3)
           if score < best_score:
              best=p.copy(); best_score=score
       else:
           no_improve+=1
           if no_improve > 2:
              alpha=max(0.03, alpha*0.65)
           if no_improve % 25 ==0:
              frac_idx=(frac_idx+1)%len(fracs)
           if no_improve > 120:
              # perturb/mix
              if rng.random()<0.5:
                 p=_normalize(best * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.04,n)))
              else:
                 p_rand=_make_osc_seed(n,float(rng.uniform(0.2,0.9)), int(rng.integers(1,6)),0.0,1.0)
                 p=_normalize(0.8*best + 0.2*p_rand)
              c=_convolve_self_fft(p)
              score=2.0*n*float(c.max())
              if score < best_score:
                 best=p.copy(); best_score=score
              no_improve=0
              alpha=0.2
              mom*=0.5
       it+=1
       if it % 100 ==0:
           print(f"ret opt n={n} it={it} score={score:.6f} best={best_score:.6f} left={deadline-time.monotonic():.1f}")
    return best,best_score
```
Candidate threshold: use `best_sc = current_score`. If p current is worse than best, we might accept a move that improves current but still >best; okay. If none improves current, no_improve. But we might want accept a move that improves best but not current? Since current >= best after reset, if current>best, we should just reset p=best when no improvement? Could add if score > best_score + 0.01 and no_improve>20: p=best. Simpler.

Need if candidate improves best but not current? current might be lower than best? impossible if best is min reached and current may be worse after noise. If current>best, candidate sc between best and current would improve current if sc<current, so accepted. Good.

In log candidate, direction L1=1; z normalized by max. But using `-alpha*z`, where z=direction/max. Since direction points to move p; for log, smaller z? Direction stored = -dmom/dn (the desired change in p direction). In direct p+alpha*direction. In log, weight change should be along direction as well? If direction_i positive means increase p_i; log update should increase weights for positive direction. Our log candidate uses `np.exp(-alpha*z)` with z=direction? That decreases weights where direction positive, opposite! Need be careful. We define direction = -dmom/dn, intended `p_new = p + alpha*direction`. For log, to mimic, we should do `w *= exp(alpha * z_scaled)` (positive where direction positive). In previous thought I had direction as negative gradient; here direction already includes negative. So log candidate should be `exp(+alpha*z)`. Let's set `_log_candidate(p, direction, alpha)` use `np.exp(alpha*z_scaled)`. But if direction has positive and negative, it moves mass. Good.

But if direction has L1=1, scaling by max can make positive max 1; alpha 0.1 moves weights up to 10% where increase. Good.

Update `_log_candidate` accordingly.

Also direct `_shift_candidate`: p + alpha*direction. Good.

```
def _make_active_mask(c, frac):
    L=c.size
    m=int(round(L*frac))
    if m<=1:
       mask=np.zeros(L); mask[int(np.argmax(c))]=1; return mask
    if m>=L: return np.ones(L)
    idx=np.argpartition(c, -m)[-m:]
    mask=np.zeros(L); mask[idx]=1.0; return mask
```

def _collect_candidates(lengths, rng):
    pool=[]; per_length=[]; counter=0
    for n in lengths:
       cands=_make_candidates(n,rng)
       scored=[]
       for p in cands:
          sc=_proxy_score_p(p)
          if np.isfinite(sc):

       scored.sort()

          per_length.append(scored[0])
          top_k = 3 if n in (2535,4096) else 2
          for item in scored[:top_k]:
             pool.append(item)
    return pool, per_length, counter

```
def _normalize(arr):
    a=np.asarray(arr,dtype=np.float64).copy()

`_convolve_self_fft`:
```
def _convolve_self_fft(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64)
    n=p.size
    if n==0: return np.array([0.0])
    L=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    F=np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F, L)[:2*n-1]
    # numerical clean
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c
```
If p contains NaN? we assume no; maybe at start ensure.

`_proxy_score_p`:
```
def _proxy_score_p(p):
    a=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return np.inf
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300: return np.inf
    a=a/s
    c=_convolve_self_fft(a)
    if c.size==0: return np.inf
    mx=float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mx): return np.inf
    return 2.0*float(a.size)*mx
```

`_correlation_active`:
```
def _correlation_active(active,p):
    n=p.size; la=active.size
    L=_next_pow2(la+n-1)
    A=np.fft.rfft(active[::-1], L)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(A*P, L)
    g=conv[n-1:la][::-1].copy()
    g=np.maximum(g,0.0) # active and p nonnegative, gradient nonnegative
    return g
```

def _shift_candidate(p,direction,alpha):
    arr=p + alpha*direction
    arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)): return None,None,np.inf
    s=float(arr.sum())
    if s <=1e-300: return None,None,np.inf
    arr=arr/s
    c=_convolve_self_fft(arr)
    sc=2.0*float(p.size)*float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(sc): return None,None,np.inf
    return arr,c,sc
```

def _make_power_seed(n, alpha=-0.5, beta=1.0):
    idx=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
    if alpha ==0.0:
       p=np.ones(n)
    else:
       p=np.power(idx+beta, alpha)
    p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
    return _normalize(p)
```
`np.power` with negative exponent and idx+beta positive fine.

`_make_osc_seed` if gamma can be >1, w negative clipped.
```
def _make_osc_seed(n, gamma=0.5, freq=1, phase=0.0, beta=0.5, alpha=-0.5):
   idx=np.arange(n)
   base=np.power(idx+beta, alpha)
   x=(idx+0.5)/float(n)
   w=1.0 + gamma*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*freq*x + phase)
   p=base*w
   p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
   return _normalize(p)
```

`_make_random_smooth`:
```
def _make_random_smooth(n,rng):
   idx=np.arange(n); x=idx/n; theta=2πx
   base=np.power(idx+0.5,-0.5)
   w=np.ones(n)
   for m in range(1,12):
      amp = 0.7/(float(m)*float(m))
      phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*np.pi))
      # maybe enforce phase 0 for lower modes? for diversity
      w += amp * np.sin(float(m)*theta + phase)
   w=np.maximum(w,0.05)
   p=base*w
   return _normalize(p)
```
`range(1,12)` constant.

`_make_random_power`:
```
def _make_random_power(n,rng):
   idx=np.arange(n)+1.0
   p=(0.1+rng.random(n))*np.power(idx,-0.35)
   return _normalize(p)
```

def _make_candidates(n,rng):
    out=[]
    for beta in (0.5,1.0,2.0): out.append(_make_power_seed(n,-0.5,beta))
    for alpha in (-0.45,-0.35,-0.25): out.append(_make_power_seed(n,alpha,1.0))
    for gamma in (0.25,0.5,0.8,1.1):
      for freq in (1,2,3,5,8):
         out.append(_make_osc_seed(n,gamma,freq,0.0,0.5,-0.5))
    for gamma in (0.5,0.8):
      for freq in (1,2):
        for phase in (np.pi/2.0, np.pi/3.0):
          out.append(_make_osc_seed(n,gamma,freq,phase,1.0,-0.5))
    for _ in range(4): out.append(_make_random_smooth(n,rng))
    for _ in range(2): out.append(_make_random_power(n,rng))
    return out
```

       results.append((bs,cnt0,n,bp))
       if bs < best_proxy: best_proxy=bs

   # if no results? add selected

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative sequences minimizing

    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2.

The program uses a set of analytic/parametric nonuniform seeds (endpoint-singular
base shapes, antisymmetric oscillatory modulations, smooth random Fourier shapes),
scores them with FFT-based convolution, then runs an FFT-local descent on the
simplex.  The final returned sequence is the best one confirmed by the exact
evaluate_sequence() wrapper, with a valid uniform-sequence fallback.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _next_pow2(m):
    y = 1
    m = int(m)
    while y < m:
        y <<= 1
    return y


def _normalize(arr):
    a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a.copy()
    bad = ~np.isfinite(a)
    if bad.any():
        a[bad] = 0.0
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if (not np.isfinite(s)) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def _convolve_self_fft(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    L = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, L)[: 2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def _proxy_score_p(p):
    a = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return np.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    a = a / s
    c = _convolve_self_fft(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    mx = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.inf
    return 2.0 * float(a.size) * mx


def _make_active_mask(c, frac):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    L = int(c.size)
    if L == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    m = int(round(float(L) * float(frac)))
    if m <= 1:
        mask = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        mask[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return mask
    if m >= L:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
    idx = np.argpartition(c, -m)[-m:]
    mask = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    mask[idx] = 1.0
    return mask


def _correlation_active(active, p):
    active = np.asarray(active, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(p.size)
    la = int(active.size)
    if n == 0 or la == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    L = _next_pow2(la + n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(active[::-1], L)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A * P, L)
    g = conv[n - 1 : la][::-1].copy()
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    return g


def _shift_candidate(p, direction, alpha):
    arr = p + alpha * direction
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        return None, None, np.inf
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return None, None, np.inf
    arr = arr / s
    c = _convolve_self_fft(arr)
    sc = 2.0 * float(p.size) * float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return None, None, np.inf
    return arr, c, sc


def _log_candidate(p, direction, alpha):
    z = np.asarray(direction, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size == 0:
        return None, None, np.inf
    mx = float(np.max(np.abs(z)))
    if mx <= 1e-300:
        return None, None, np.inf
    z = z / mx
    w = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    fac = np.exp(alpha * z)
    w = w * fac
    w = np.maximum(w, 1e-300)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)):
        return None, None, np.inf
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return None, None, np.inf
    arr = w / s
    c = _convolve_self_fft(arr)
    sc = 2.0 * float(p.size) * float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return None, None, np.inf
    return arr, c, sc


def _make_power_seed(n, alpha=-0.5, beta=1.0):
    n = int(n)
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if abs(float(alpha)) < 1e-15:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p = np.power(idx + float(beta), float(alpha))
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    return _normalize(p)


def _make_osc_seed(n, gamma=0.5, freq=1, phase=0.0, beta=0.5, alpha=-0.5):
    n = int(n)
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    base = np.power(idx + float(beta), float(alpha))
    x = (idx + 0.5) / float(n)
    w = 1.0 + float(gamma) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(freq) * x + float(phase))
    p = base * w
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    return _normalize(p)


def _make_random_smooth(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = idx / float(n)
    theta = 2.0 * np.pi * x
    base = np.power(idx + 0.5, -0.5)
    w = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, 12):
        amp = 0.7 / (float(m) * float(m))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        w += amp * np.sin(float(m) * theta + phase)
    w = np.maximum(w, 0.05)
    p = base * w
    return _normalize(p)


def _make_random_power(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 1.0
    p = (0.1 + rng.random(n)) * np.power(idx, -0.35)
    return _normalize(p)


def _make_candidates(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    out = []

    # Basic endpoint-singular and slightly regularized shapes.
    for beta in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        out.append(_make_power_seed(n, -0.5, beta))
    for alpha in (-0.45, -0.35, -0.25):
        out.append(_make_power_seed(n, alpha, 1.0))

    # Antisymmetric oscillatory modulation of the singular shape.
    for gamma in (0.25, 0.5, 0.8, 1.1):
        for freq in (1, 2, 3, 5, 8):
            out.append(_make_osc_seed(n, gamma, freq, 0.0, 0.5, -0.5))

    # More phase variants.
    for gamma in (0.5, 0.8):
        for freq in (1, 2):
            for phase in (np.pi / 2.0, np.pi / 3.0):
                out.append(_make_osc_seed(n, gamma, freq, phase, 1.0, -0.5))

    # Smooth random Fourier perturbations and random endpoint-weighted vectors.
    for _ in range(4):
        out.append(_make_random_smooth(n, rng))
    for _ in range(2):
        out.append(_make_random_power(n, rng))

    return out


def _optimize_p(p0, n, budget_s, rng):
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    p = _normalize(np.asarray(p0, dtype=np.float64))
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64), np.inf

    c = _convolve_self_fft(p)
    score = 2.0 * float(n) * float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        c = _convolve_self_fft(p)
        score = 2.0 * float(n) * float(c.max())

    best = p.copy()
    best_score = score

    if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        return best, best_score

    fracs = (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.40)
    frac_idx = 0
    alpha = 0.20
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if score > best_score + 0.05 and no_improve > 60:
            p = best.copy()
            c = _convolve_self_fft(p)
            score = 2.0 * float(n) * float(c.max())
            no_improve = 0
            continue

        active = _make_active_mask(c, fracs[frac_idx])
        grad = _correlation_active(active, p)
        h = grad - float(np.mean(grad))

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
            frac_idx = (frac_idx + 1) % len(fracs)
            no_improve += 5
            if no_improve > 20:
                p = best.copy()
                c = _convolve_self_fft(p)
                score = 2.0 * float(n) * float(c.max())
                no_improve = 0
            continue

        l1 = float(np.sum(np.abs(h)))
        if l1 <= 1e-14:
            frac_idx = (frac_idx + 1) % len(fracs)
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 30:
                p = best.copy()
                c = _convolve_self_fft(p)
                score = 2.0 * float(n) * float(c.max())
                no_improve = 0
            continue

        mom = 0.55 * mom + 0.45 * h
        dmom = mom - float(np.mean(mom))
        dn = float(np.sum(np.abs(dmom)))
        if dn <= 1e-14:
            dmom = h
            dn = l1

        direction = -dmom / dn

        current_score = score
        best_sc = current_score
        best_cand = None
        best_cc = None

        # Direct simplex step line search.
        for a in (alpha * 0.25, alpha * 0.5, alpha, alpha * 2.0):
            if a <= 0.0 or a > 5.0:
                continue
            cand, cc, sc = _shift_candidate(p, direction, a)
            if cand is not None and sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best_sc = sc
                best_cand = cand
                best_cc = cc

        # Log-space step as a safer positive-domain alternative.
        if best_cand is None or best_sc > current_score - 1e-5:
            for lg in (0.10, 0.05):
                cand, cc, sc = _log_candidate(p, direction, lg)
                if cand is not None and sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_cand = cand
                    best_cc = cc

        if best_cand is not None and best_sc < current_score - 1e-15:
            p = best_cand
            c = best_cc
            score = best_sc
            no_improve = 0
            alpha = min(2.0, alpha * 1.3)
            if score < best_score:
                best = p.copy()
                best_score = score
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 2:
                alpha = max(0.03, alpha * 0.65)
            if no_improve % 25 == 0:
                frac_idx = (frac_idx + 1) % len(fracs)
            if no_improve > 120:
                if rng.random() < 0.5:
                    p = _normalize(best * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.04, n)))
                else:
                    rr = _make_osc_seed(
                        n,
                        float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.9)),
                        int(rng.integers(1, 6)),
                        0.0,
                        1.0,
                        -0.5,
                    )
                    p = _normalize(0.8 * best + 0.2 * rr)
                c = _convolve_self_fft(p)
                score = 2.0 * float(n) * float(c.max())
                if score < best_score:
                    best = p.copy()
                    best_score = score
                no_improve = 0
                alpha = 0.20
                mom *= 0.5

        it += 1
        if it % 200 == 0:
            left = deadline - time.monotonic()
            print(
                f"optimize n={n} it={it} score={score:.8f} "
                f"best={best_score:.8f} left={left:.1f}",
                flush=True,
            )
            if left < 2.0:
                break

    return best, float(best_score)


def _exact_eval_list(seq_list):
    try:
        return float(evaluate_sequence(seq_list))
    except Exception:
        return np.inf


def _valid_list(p):
    a = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    bad = ~np.isfinite(a)
    if bad.any():
        a = a.copy()
        a[bad] = 0.0
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return [float(x) for x in a]


def _propose_impl(seed, budget_s):
    start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(0.0, float(budget_s))

    if total_budget < 30.0:
        return [1.0] * 1024

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    if total_budget > 2000.0:
        lengths = [1024, 2048, 2535, 4096]
    elif total_budget > 1200.0:
        lengths = [1024, 2048, 2535]
    else:
        lengths = [1024, 2048]

    height_p = None
    height_n = 0
    height_counter = -1

    try:
        h = height_sequence_1
        ha = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)
        if ha.ndim == 1 and ha.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(ha)):
            hn = int(ha.size)
            if 128 <= hn <= 8192:
                hp = _normalize(ha)
                hsc = _proxy_score_p(hp)
                if np.isfinite(hsc):
                    height_p = hp
                    height_n = hn
    except NameError:
        pass

    candidates = []
    per_length = []
    counter = 0

    for n in lengths:
        cands = _make_candidates(int(n), rng)
        scored = []
        for p in cands:
            sc = _proxy_score_p(p)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                scored.append((sc, counter, int(n), p))
                counter += 1
        if scored:
            scored.sort()
            per_length.append(scored[0])
            top_k = 3 if int(n) == 2535 else 2
            for item in scored[:top_k]:
                candidates.append(item)

    if height_p is not None:
        hsc = _proxy_score_p(height_p)
        if np.isfinite(hsc):
            height_counter = counter
            candidates.append((hsc, height_counter, height_n, height_p))
            per_length.append((hsc, height_counter, height_n, height_p))
            counter += 1

    if not candidates:
        return [1.0] * 1024

    candidates.sort()

    selected = []
    used = []
    for item in per_length:
        if item[1] not in used:
            selected.append(item)
            used.append(item[1])
    for item in candidates:
        if len(selected) >= 7:
            break
        if item[1] not in used:
            selected.append(item)
            used.append(item[1])

    if not selected:
        return [1.0] * 1024

    # Reserve time for final exact evaluation and safety.
    final_reserve = 120.0 if total_budget > 600.0 else max(10.0, total_budget * 0.25)
    opt_total = max(30.0, total_budget - final_reserve)
    overall_deadline = start + opt_total

    base_w = [0.34, 0.20, 0.14, 0.10, 0.08, 0.06, 0.08]
    wsum = float(sum(base_w[: len(selected)]))
    if wsum <= 0.0:
        wsum = float(len(selected))

    results = []

    for idx, item in enumerate(selected):
        sc0, cnt0, n, p = item
        if time.monotonic() >= overall_deadline:
            break

        alloc = opt_total * float(base_w[idx]) / wsum
        alloc = min(alloc, max(0.0, overall_deadline - time.monotonic() - 2.0))
        if alloc < 10.0:
            remaining_items = max(1, len(selected) - idx)
            alloc = min(opt_total / float(remaining_items), max(0.0, overall_deadline - time.monotonic() - 2.0))
        if alloc < 10.0:
            break

        bp, bs = _optimize_p(p, int(n), alloc, rng)
        results.append((float(bs), int(cnt0), int(n), bp))
        print(f"search done n={n} proxy={bs:.8f}", flush=True)

    if not results:
        for item in selected:
            results.append(item)

    final_candidates = []
    final_seen = []
    for item in results:
        if item[1] not in final_seen:
            final_candidates.append(item)
            final_seen.append(item[1])

    # Uniform fallbacks, guaranteed score 2 (up to roundoff).
    final_candidates.append((2.0, counter, 1024, np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)))
    counter += 1
    final_candidates.append((2.0, counter, 256, np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)))
    counter += 1

    if height_p is not None and height_counter not in final_seen:
        hsc = _proxy_score_p(height_p)
        final_candidates.append((float(hsc), height_counter, height_n, height_p))

    final_candidates.sort()

    final_deadline = start + max(0.0, total_budget - 5.0)

    best_seq = [1.0] * 1024
    best_exact = _exact_eval_list(best_seq)
    if not np.isfinite(best_exact):
        best_exact = 1e100

    evaluated = 0
    for item in final_candidates:
        if time.monotonic() >= final_deadline:
            break
        if evaluated > 12:
            break
        sc, cnt, n, p = item
        if int(n) > 20000:
            continue
        seq = _valid_list(p)
        est = _exact_eval_list(seq)
        evaluated += 1
        if np.isfinite(est) and est < best_exact:
            best_exact = est
            best_seq = seq
        print(f"exact n={n} score={est:.8f} best={best_exact:.8f}", flush=True)
        if best_exact <= 1.0 + 1e-7:
            break

    return best_seq


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    try:
        return _propose_impl(int(seed), float(budget_s))
    except Exception:
        return [1.0] * 1024
```
